data-pagefind-ignore="">#</span></a></h2><p>Feature engineering remains one of the most impactful aspects of machine learning projects. While automated tools and deep learning have reduced some of the manual work, understanding the principles and techniques outlined in this guide will help you create more effective models and gain deeper insights into your data.</p><p>The key to successful feature engineering lies in combining domain expertise with statistical knowledge, systematic experimentation, and rigorous validation. Start with simple transformations, build complexity gradually, and always validate your improvements through proper cross-validation techniques.</p><p>Remember that feature engineering is an iterative process. Continuously monitor your features’ performance, adapt to changing data patterns, and maintain comprehensive documentation to ensure your models remain robust and interpretable over time.</p><p>By mastering these advanced feature engineering techniques, you’ll be well-equipped to tackle complex machine learning challenges and extract maximum value from your data, regardless of the domain or application you’re working with.</p></section></section>  </div> <script type="module">document.addEventListener("click",function(s){const t=s.target;if(t&&t.classList.contains("copy-btn")){const c=t.closest("pre")?.querySelector("code"),i=Array.from(c?.querySelectorAll(".code:not(summary *)")??[]).map(e=>e.textContent).map(e=>e===`

Variables vs Constants<a class="anchor" href="#3-variables-vs-constants"><span class="anchor-icon" data-pagefind-ignore="">#</span></a></h3><div class="expressive-code"><figure class="frame"><figcaption class="header"></figcaption><pre data-language="python" class="wrap" style="--ecMaxLine:50ch"><code><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">1</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Constants are immutable</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">2</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">constant_tensor </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tf.constant([</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">])</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">3</div></div><div class="code">
</div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">4</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Variables are mutable and trainable</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">5</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">variable_tensor </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tf.Variable([</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">1.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">2.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">3.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">])</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">6</div></div><div class="code">
</div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">7</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Update variable</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">8</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">variable_tensor.assign([</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">4.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">5.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">6.0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">])</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">9</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">f</span><span style="--0:#9ECBFF;--1:#9ECBFF">"Updated variable: </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">variable_tensor</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--0:#9ECBFF;--1:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">10</div></div><div class="code">
</div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">11</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Variables are used for model </div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">8</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Compute gradient dy/dx</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">9</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">gradient </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tape.gradient(y, x)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">10</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">f</span><span style="--0:#9ECBFF;--1:#9ECBFF">"Gradient at x=3: </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">gradient</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--0:#9ECBFF;--1:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">)  </span><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Should be 2*3 + 2 = 8</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">11</div></div><div class="code">

href="#building-your-first-neural-network"><span class="anchor-icon" data-pagefind-ignore="">#</span></a></h2><section><h3 id="1-linear-regression-example">1. Linear Regression Example<a class="anchor" href="#1-linear-regression-example"><span class="anchor-icon" data-pagefind-ignore="">#</span></a></h3><p>Let’s start with a simple linear regression problem:</p><div class="expressive-code"><figure class="frame"><figcaption class="header"></figcaption><pre data-language="python" class="wrap" style="--ecMaxLine:66ch"><code><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">1</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">import</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> matplotlib.pyplot </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">as</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> plt</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">2</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">import</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> numpy </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">as</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> np</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">3</div></div><div class="code">
</div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">4</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Generate synthetic data</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">5</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">np.random.seed(</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">42</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">6</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">X </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> np.linspace(</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">100</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">).reshape(</span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">-</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">7</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">y </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">*</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> X.flatten() </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">+</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">+</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> np.random.normal(</span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">0.5</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">100</span><span </div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">9</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#99A0A6;--1:#99A0A6"># Convert to TensorFlow tensors</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">10</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">X_tf </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tf.constant(X, </span><span style="--0:#FFAB70;--1:#FFAB70">dtype</span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">tf.float32)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">11</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">y_tf </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tf.constant(y, </span><span style="--0:#FFAB70;--1:#FFAB70">dtype</span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">tf.float32)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">12</div></div><div class="code">
--
</div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">29</div></div><div class="code"><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">for</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> epoch </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">in</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> </span><span style="--0:#79B8FF;--1:#79B8FF">range</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">(epochs):</span></div></div><div class="ec-line" style="--ecIndent:4ch"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">30</div></div><div class="code"><span class="indent">    </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">with</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tf.GradientTape() </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">as</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> tape:</span></div></div><div class="ec-line" style="--ecIndent:8ch"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">31</div></div><div class="code"><span class="indent"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">        </span></span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">predictions </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> linear_model(X_tf)</span></div></div><div class="ec-line" style="--ecIndent:8ch"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">32</div></div><div class="code"><span class="indent"><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">        </span></span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8">loss </span><span style="--0:#F97583;--1:#F97583">=</span><span style="--0:#E1E4E8;--1:#E1E4E8"> mse_loss(y_tf, tf.squeeze(predictions))</span></div></div><div class="ec-line"><div class="gutter"><div class="ln" aria-hidden="true">33</div></div><div class="code">
